## ADR-48 — Editing a saved store invoice draft at Invoices Edit, versioned and idempotent, with sealed conflicts and no-ops (DECIDED — M7B-INVOICES-P2)

**Context.** After ADR-47 and its M7B-INVOICES-P1-R2 amendments a store draft could be saved, listed and reopened read-only, and migration 028 pinned its version to 1. For this stage the owner decided that a saved draft can be edited now, that editing needs Invoices at Edit or above, that every edit names the version it read, that USD stays, and that discarding and deleting a draft stay deferred. The Customer directory's edit hold store still followed the sign-in state rather than the session end (DA-200's remainder).

**Decision.**
1. **One permission, one decision.** `edit_invoice_drafts`: Invoices at Edit or above on the store grant's role, decidable at store scope only, with no Customers corequisite (a Customers threshold would be a new policy and would refuse a line-only edit keeping the current customer). One requirement object, `INVOICE_DRAFT_EDIT_REQUIREMENT`, serves the edit and its eligibility read; the evaluator, authorizer and revalidator are unchanged, and migration 029's routine decides the same threshold. Built-in defaults: `store_owner` and `manager` pass; `technician` (View) and `sales_staff` (Create) are refused; owner-edited levels decide. Residual: DA-203.
2. **The draft's own edit eligibility.** `GET /api/v1/stores/{storeId}/invoice-drafts/{id}/edit-eligibility` is decided by the authorizer alone and reads no draft, so every well-formed id gets the same answer; exactly `200 { "eligibility": "allowed" }`, otherwise the creation eligibility's answers. It is a snapshot; the PATCH decides again. The creation eligibility is unchanged byte for byte and proves Create only.
3. **The command.** `PATCH /api/v1/stores/{storeId}/invoice-drafts/{id}` is the typed command `store.invoice_draft.update`: a closed body `{ expectedTenant, expectedVersion, customerId, lines }` that replaces the customer and the complete ordered lines (no per-line merge), the save's line contract through one shared validator, the server's subtotal, at most 64 KiB, and `200 { draftId, version, changed }`.
4. **Conflicts are sealed per operation.** A stale version, a stale no-op included, is the sealed `409 write_conflict`; a draft whose state admits no edit the sealed `409 invalid_state` (unreachable while 028 pins the status); a current-version no-op the sealed `200` with `changed: false` and no business row, version, audit record or event (its idempotency completion is still written); a missing or foreign draft and a missing or foreign customer one unsealed `404 not_found`; another `expectedTenant` the unsealed `409 write_conflict` (the save's precondition answer, widened to this command by its kind). The sealed answers rest on the routine's unlocked level decision (DA-204). *(M7B-INVOICES-P2-R1: that sentence no longer holds. Every sealed answer is decided after the role's guard key is taken SHARED and held until COMMIT, for manager, technician and sales_staff; store_owner takes no key. DA-204 is closed for the runtime path while migration 030's trigger is enabled.)* An unknown outcome is recovered only with the same key and bytes.
5. **The fingerprint's target is the draft.** A store template with `{id}` binds the path's draft id as the target, with the trusted tenant, store and actor beside it; the save keeps binding the store, and `IDEMPOTENCY_CONTRACT_VERSION` is unchanged. The id must have the outbox's aggregate-id shape (DA-206).
6. **Audit and event.** A real edit writes one audit record (action `store.invoice_draft.update`, scope `store`, the draft as `subject_invoice_draft_id`) and one outbox event `invoice_draft.updated` v1 `{ storeId, customerId, lineCount }` at the new version; a no-op, a conflict and a refusal write neither.
7. **Migration 029.** The CHECK `version between 1 and 9007199254740991` under its 028 name; the row trigger `trg_invoice_draft_immutable` (BEFORE UPDATE, ENABLE ALWAYS), which keeps the id, tenant, store, creation time and creator fixed and lets the version move only by 0 or +1 on every path, the table owner's included; the definer routine `m7b_invoice_draft_update`, EXECUTE for `tmpos_app` only, with the lock order its header gives (the draft header `FOR UPDATE` filtered by id, tenant and store only, the customer `FOR KEY SHARE`, the role row `FOR SHARE` on the change path only) and the reason it cannot deadlock. The role anchor is written only on the change path, so a sealed answer writes no business row, version, role anchor, audit record or outbox event — only the sealed idempotency completion (DA-36; DA-205 records 024's contrary order). No table or schema privilege changes. *(M7B-INVOICES-P2-R1, the amendment of this decision: the routine that runs is `tmpos_invoice_edit.m7b_invoice_draft_update_v2` from migration 030, and 029's routine is no longer executable by `tmpos_app`. Its order is the draft header `FOR UPDATE`, the role's guard key `SHARED` (not for store_owner), the customer `FOR KEY SHARE`, then the lines; it locks no Invoices role row. THE RULE (030's header): a transaction holding the key SHARED (an edit) never locks an Invoices role or version row after taking it; a writer taking the key EXCLUSIVE writes after it only the version row of the role row it already holds (021's upsert; on a role's first level change that row is inserted after the key), beside its own audit, outbox and completion rows. The setter takes its role row, and its version row when one exists, before the key; 030's header gives the argument. A sealed non-change answer writes nothing but its idempotency completion, and the edit creates no role anchor.)*
8. **One snapshot per read.** 028's `STABLE` get is kept: a read overlapping an edit returns the complete earlier or the complete later draft, shown against a `VOLATILE` copy that mixes them.
9. **A rollback never resets a version.** 029's down refuses while any draft's version is above 1; retained edit audit records and outbox rows do not block it, because a guard on append-only evidence would refuse every later shared-CI rollback and protect nothing.
10. **No timestamp policy.** No modified-at column is added.
11. **The shop's editor (commit `4f3c700`).** Edit is offered only on the edit eligibility's exact `allowed` for the user, generation, store, draft and request. Save sends one PATCH per press with the attempt frozen first; "Saved" or "No changes were needed" only after the 200, then one read-back; a conflict is reviewed explicitly ("Reapply my changes" under a new key, "Discard my changes" locally); an unknown outcome is re-sent only by "Check again" with the same key and bytes, and is held only while the editor stays open (DA-208). A new generation, a session end and a sign-out close the editor synchronously; a late answer changes nothing.

**Alternatives not taken.**
- (a) *A Customers View corequisite.* A new policy that would refuse a line-only edit; its absence is recorded as DA-203.
- (b) *A per-line merge PATCH.* Ambiguous under concurrent edits; the edit replaces the complete ordered lines.
- (c) *A version filter in the header lock's WHERE.* Under READ COMMITTED a waiter would re-evaluate it on the committed row and answer `not_found` instead of `conflict`.
- (d) *A new or `VOLATILE` read.* The unchanged `STABLE` get already gives one snapshot.
- (e) *The role anchor before the sealed decisions.* 024's order; it would make a sealed answer leave a role row behind (DA-36, DA-205).
- (f) *A rollback guard on audit or outbox evidence, or a version reset.* The first protects nothing and blocks shared-CI rollbacks; the second would let a stale editor overwrite a newer change.
- (g) *Reusing the creation eligibility to offer Edit.* It proves Create only.

**Consequences.**
- **Closed.** DA-200 in full (commit `dc48862`). Nothing at gate level: G-IAM, G-PERSIST, G-AUDIT, G-IDEMPOT, G-DBROLE, G-MIGRATE, G-CPLOGIN, G-STATUS, G-ISOLATION and G-PRIVACY stay OPEN.
- **Behaviour.** A team member with a store grant at Invoices Edit or above can edit a saved USD draft's customer and lines; two editors of one version get one 200 and one sealed 409; a level narrowing, a grant suspension or a store status change committed first refuses the edit, and one attempted while the edit holds its locks waits for its COMMIT.
- **Supersedes or narrows.** ADR-47 d2, d8, d9, Behaviour, Deploy and rollback, Register and Still open; ADR-46 d5, d7 and Behaviour; ADR-42 d5 (extended) and d11 (made true by `dc48862`).
- **Deploy and rollback.** The runtime and 029 move together (preflight: exactly 001–029 and one new EXECUTE grant); the 001–028 runtime refuses a 029 database and this one a 028 one. 029's up and down lock seven tables `ACCESS EXCLUSIVE` before any check, a maintenance window. Roll 029 back before 028. Checksums: up `34a4073fb494fe2ac8d81e2ca8b706b56ece70ea29073f88f1787f194ce780b2`, down `12bd3b092cde24b17cc7cc7ec8b81f5adc4b4bc95c92d0e75d9a94ef4d8b731a`. *(M7B-INVOICES-P2-R1: the runtime and 030 move together. The startup preflight requires exactly 001–030 and the edit's new EXECUTE and the schema's USAGE in place of 029's EXECUTE; the 001–029 runtime refuses a 030 database and this one a 029 database. 030 rolls back first, restoring 029's routine grant and its DA-204 window. Its checksums are in [05](./05-canonical-data-ownership-and-api-db-contracts.md) §2.)*
- **Register.** [08](./08-production-gate-and-risk-register.md): DA-200 closed in full; DA-36, DA-118, DA-120, DA-159, DA-183, DA-184, DA-186 (editing delivered; the rest of the lifecycle stays open), DA-187, DA-190 and DA-197 amended; DA-199, DA-201 and DA-202 corrected; DA-203 to DA-209 new. ADR-42, ADR-46 and ADR-47 amended. *(M7B-INVOICES-P2-R1: DA-204 closed for the runtime edit path; DA-36, DA-118, DA-120, DA-203, DA-207 and DA-208 amended; DA-205, DA-206 and DA-209 unchanged; DA-210 new and open, for the draft save's own window in frozen 028.)*
- **Still open (owner decisions).**
  - discarding and deleting drafts; issuing and numbering; tax, discounts, shipping and payment; fractional quantities; catalog price authority (DA-186);
  - draft retention and privacy before live use (DA-187);
  - the mapping of legacy browser customers (DA-176);
  - store provisioning, grant management and any tenant-to-store inheritance (DA-188);
  - store-scope customer reads or a tenant-grant requirement for drafts (DA-189), and whether an Invoices Edit role without Customers View may name a customer (DA-203);
  - whether an edit's unknown outcome should outlive its editor (DA-208);
  - whether the draft save needs the same guard as the edit (DA-210).
- **Evidence.** Disposable PostgreSQL, local sockets and a real browser only ([05](./05-canonical-data-ownership-and-api-db-contracts.md) §2 "Invoices — editing a saved draft"):
  - `tests/db/invoiceDraftEditRoutine.integration.test.mjs` IDE-01 to IDE-15: 029's apply and grants, the trigger, real edits, sealed and rolled-back answers writing nothing in the routine, every bound, one snapshot against a `VOLATILE` control, ordering by lock waits, the down;
  - `tests/db/invoiceDraftEditHttp.integration.test.mjs` IEH-01 to IEH-14 over two tenant entries: authority and the edit eligibility, the sealed no-op and 409s, the non-enumerating 404, faults, key reuse, both lost-COMMIT branches, revocations ordered against the commit, the 64 KiB bound, logs;
  - `server/runtime/invoiceDraftEdit.test.ts` IEU-01 to IEU-08 and `server/composition/tenantAuthorization.test.ts` M7B-AZ-04 and M7B-AZ-05; main runner 168 suites at `573de6c`;
  - the shop: the client's IDC-05 and IDC-19 to IDC-23 under the Node test runner (no DOM), the section's SID-77 to SID-111 in jsdom; in Chromium SDB-11 to SDB-15 (which context changes are proven where: DA-209); the Customer directory's cleanup in jsdom SCD-177 to SCD-186 and, as a regression guard, in Chromium SSE-04; frontend ratchet 914 cases and shop browser gate 55 scenarios at `4f3c700`. *(M7B-INVOICES-P2-R1: `tests/db/invoiceDraftEditRoutine.integration.test.mjs` runs IDE-01 to IDE-22 except IDE-15, which IDE-19 replaces; IDE-17 and IDE-20 ordering and writer participation, IDE-18 the fixed owner (no key), IDE-19 liveness after the key wait, IDE-21 rollback and upgrade; IDE-11, IDE-16 and IDE-22 the DA-204 reproductions, labelled R in the test files' headers; `tests/db/invoiceDraftEditHttp.integration.test.mjs` runs IEH-01 to IEH-17, with IEH-15 and IEH-16 ordering, IEH-17 replay and current authority (AC-09), and IEH-08 extended. The 404 is one identical body that hides which reference check failed.)*
